# AUTO-GENERATED MACHINE REFACTORING LAYERS

```logos-spec
CONSTANT SVE_L3002_auto_generated_Context : Scope
VARIABLE SVE_L3002_auto_generated_Assertion : Prop

DEF SVE_L3002_auto_generated.check (s : Scope) : Prop :=
  ASSERT_CONTEXT_BOUND(s) ⟹ THROW(LOGOS_ERR_GENERIC, "Legacy uncodified verification checkpoint reached.")
```
